先週（10/26～10/30）発表の主な経済指標
AI関連を中心に設備投資が増勢を維持し、産業用機械にも広
がり。好調な企業収益も投資を後押し。個人消費も堅調。金
融所得や移転所得、株高による資産効果が購買力を下支え
政策要因を除く都区部CPIは上昇基調。財価格の上昇が目立
つ一方、サービス価格改定の広がりは限定的。企業の価格転嫁
は進展も、消費マインドの弱さが値上げの重荷に

のコアPCE物価は3％を超える伸びが継続。景気の
底堅さを背景にFRBにもう一段の金融引き締め余地

労働分配率は低下傾向。賃金
以外の所得が家計所得を補完
米国：企業収益が拡大する一方、労働分配率は低下。金融所得や移転所得が消費を下支え
 企業収益が好調を維持する一方、労働所得の対GDP比は低下傾向。金融所得や政府からの移転所得が家計所得を補完
― 株式配当や社会保障給付など、賃金以外の所得が家計の購買力を支える構図
 こうした所得環境に加え、株高による資産効果も消費を下支え。10月入り後も個人消費は底堅く推移
― 燃料高にもかかわらず、空港利用者数は前年並みの水準を維持し、小売売上高も高めの伸びを維持
― 年末商戦も前年を上回る伸びが予想されている

日本：コアCPIは10月に鈍化も、政策要因を除けばインフレ率は上昇基調
4
 10月の都区部コアＣＰＩは前年比＋2.6％（前月：同＋2.7％）と伸び鈍化
― 無償化期間の時期ズレによる9月水道料金の一時的な押し上げが剥落したことに加え、コメ価格の下落が影響
 ただし、政策要因を除けばインフレ率は上昇基調。サービス物価が底堅く推移する中、財物価の加速がインフレ率押し上げ
― 原油高等によるコスト増の転嫁が進展し、エネルギー、その他財（除く水道料金）の価格上昇が足元で加速
― 春先に鈍化した一般サービス物価（除く家賃）も人件費等の転嫁進展を映じて前年比＋2％近傍で推移

―10月は外食を中心に価格改定の動きがみられるも、広がりは限定的。一方、外食除く改定品目では値上げ幅が拡大
（注） 食品は、コメと外食を除く食料価格。水道料金はその他財に該当。サービスは一般
サービスと公共サービスの合計（家賃を除く）

日本：企業の価格転嫁は進展も、消費マインドの弱さが追加値上げの重荷に
5
 企業の価格転嫁は着実に進展
― 日商LOBO調査では、コスト増加分の4割以上を価格転嫁できた企業が55.4％と4月から2.6％ポイント増加。ただし、労
務費の転嫁は40.6％にとどまり、販売単価DIも小幅低下するなど、中小企業では追加値上げの勢いに一服感
 消費者態度指数は2カ月連続で悪化。消費マインドの弱さは残存
― 物価高による節約志向が企業の追加値上げを制約している可能性。日銀は基調的な物価上昇率が2％に到達したと判
断し、上振れリスクへの警戒を強化。ただし、サービス価格の改定に広がりはみられず
（出所） 日本商工会議所「LOBO調査」より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

### Page 7

米国：10月FOMC～据え置き、ウォーシュ議長は引き締めがAIセクターにも影響との認識示す
6
 3.75～4.00％に据え置き（2人が反対、利上げを支持）。景気評価を上方修正しつつ、9月利上げ効果を見極め
― 景気評価は「堅調」から「力強い」へ。ウォーシュ議長は投資の広がりに言及
 AIセクターでは投資の選別が起きており、高い成長期待を持つ同セクターに対しても金融政策は有効との認識示す
 2027・2028年の会合の一部を政策決定を行わず、声明文も公表しない戦略的議論の会合とすると表明
AIセクター、生産性、タスクフォース声明文の変更、政策判断、会合運営
 反対2票は利上げを主張
 景気評価は「堅調」から「力強い」へ
 様子見による「情報のさらなる蓄積」
が、物価安定への「適時の復帰」のため
の政策判断に役立つ
声明文
 「以前より引き締まった」が「引き締め
的な水準にあるわけではない」と述べ、
追加利上げの余地残す
 「参加者の多数派は利上げ効果が出てい
ると認識」と言及

金融環境
と効果
 「政治は、この決定に何の役割も果たし
ていない」と言明 中間選挙
 2027年秋の1回、2028年には2回の会合
を政策決定せず議論だけの会合へ
 戦略的な熟議のための時間と余地確保
会合運営
 「投資家が期待する収益率はあまりに違
うので、AIには何の影響も及ばない」と
いう見方を否定
 「新技術と今回の投資急増」に対して金
融政策は有効
 信用スプレッドの拡大などは「市場参加
者が、どのような投資を行いたいか、資
本配分を選別している」ことの表れ
AI投資
 「経済全体の生産性は、ここ2、3年ほど
上向いて」おり、高い経済成⾧率によっ
てインフレ圧力が生まれ、利上げが必要
な状況ではないとの認識
生産性
11月16日に生産性・雇用の公聴会。タスク
フォース報告は年末が目安（一部は遅延の
可能性）。バランスシート提言は2027年1
～3月期の早期を想定

### Page 8

内容ポイント
 26年3Qの景気は引き続き拡大
 エネルギー価格と資金調達コストの上昇が今後の重しに景気
 エネルギー価格の見通しは9月の予想を上回っている
 今後数四半期の物価は目標を大幅に上回る見通し。その後
はエネルギー価格低下と金利上昇により目標水準に回帰へ
 物価の基調・賃金はおおむね安定。ただし、価格形成の初
期段階では上昇圧力がみられる
 今後6週間で得られる、基調的なインフレに関する多くの情
報を、とりわけ注視していく
物価
 9月理事会以降、金融環境は引き締まっている。金利は上昇
し、株価は下落している
 期待インフレの上昇やハイパースケーラーの社債発行等、
複合的な要因が金利を押し上げ。加えて、債務の多い加盟
国は固有の課題に直面
 市場の不当かつ無秩序な動向が金融政策伝達の妨げとな
る場合、対応手段は用意されている
金融
環境
 全会一致で金利据え置きも、利上げが適切かどうか議論し
た。「今利上げを実施してはどうか」との意見もあった
 今後の金融政策はデータに基づき、会合ごとに決定

（「増加」－「減少」、％）
欧州：ECBは政策金利を据え置き。高金利は企業の資金需要を押し下げ
7
 ECBは預金ファシリティ金利を2.5％で据え置き。利上げが議論されるも物価の基調に著変なく、慎重な対応が適切と判断
― 利上げの要否検討にあたり、エネルギー価格の上昇と金融環境の引き締まりが主な論点に。今後は物価の基調に関す
る指標を特に注視する姿勢。ラガルド総裁は、進むべき方向が「きわめて明確」であると発言
― ラガルド総裁は2027年2月に早期退任へ。同年5月までに専務理事2名を含む計3名の幹部が交代する見込み
 10月の銀行貸出調査によれば、金利水準が企業の資金需要減少に寄与。高金利の影響は既に一部で顕在化
― 同調査によれば、企業向け貸出基準はフランスで一段と厳格化。銀行のリスク許容度低下やリスク認識の高まりが主因

企業向け銀行貸出の需要増減要因
（注） 需要増減は、企業向け貸出の需要が前四半期から「かなり増加」「やや増加」と回
答した銀行の割合から、「かなり減少」「やや減少」と回答した銀行の割合を引いた
もの。「その他要因」には他の調達手段による代替や、「運転資金・在庫」「設備投
資」以外の資金需要発生等を含む。2026年Q3の調査期間は9月21日～10月6日
（出所）ECBより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
10月ECB理事会のポイント（声明文・総裁会見）
（出所）ECBより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
金利水準の高さが
貸出需要を下押し

### Page 9

英国：政府は財政規律を重視しつつエネルギー高対策に注力。財政余力は縮小
 英政府は秋季財政計画を公表。財政規律を重視しつつ家計や小規模事業者向け支援等を打ち出す内容
― 燃料税減税と電気料金へのVAT減税の延長、給付金支給によるエネルギー高対策、住宅購入支援などの家計支援策
を発表。小規模事業者の税負担軽減や電気料金の引き下げなど、企業向け支援も公表
― 上記政策の財源は銀行への追加課税やギャンブルへの課税強化等で確保する方針
― 防衛費は2030年までにGDP比3％へ引き上げる方針。ただし、財源を含む詳細は来年の歳出見直しに先送り
 財政余力は前回3月の237億ポンドから147億ポンドに大幅縮小。中東情勢悪化に伴う金利上昇等が背景
英国：財政余力（左）と3月以降の財政余力減少要因（右）
（出所）OBRより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
英国：秋季財政計画の主な内容
（出所） 英国政府より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
• 燃料税5ペンス減税や電気料金のVA T免除を来年7月まで実施
• 2,000 万世帯を対象に100ポンドのエネルギー高支援給付金支給

• 使用量にかかわらず課される家庭用ガスのスタンディングチャー
ジ廃止
• エネルギー集約型製造業1万社の電気代を20～25％軽減
• 手ごろな住宅建設に15億ポンド追加拠出。住宅初回購入者向け
支援「Your First Home」に47億ポンドを充当
• 小規模事業者の事業用不動産税免除対象を拡大。パブや音楽会
場等には同税を追加で20％軽減
• 食品等100件以上の品目の関税撤廃
• 防衛投資計画で必要とされていた150億ポンドの追加投資全額を
拠出。2030年に防衛費GDP比3％達成を企図。来年の歳出見直
しで財源の詳細公表
• 地方分権の一環として、来年から事業用不動産税のうち20％を地
方に移転、2030年までに100％への引き上げを目指すほか、所得
税も2028年から一部を地方に配分しインフラ整備の財源に
• 銀行への上乗せ課税を3％から6％に引き上げ
• 高額賭博機へのギャンブル税を20％から25％に引き上げ
• 貯蓄・不動産所得の税率を2％Pt引き上げ

英国：財政懸念を背景とする金利上昇リスクはくすぶり続ける見通し
 財政計画がおおむね事前の予想どおりの内容であったことなどから英10年債利回りの動きは限定的。ただし、足元の高金
利の要因であるインフレや財政懸念、世界的なAI関連投資増加は今後も続くとみられ、金利は高止まりが予想される

 利払い費による財政圧迫継続が予想される中、財政懸念を背景とする一段の金利上昇リスクはくすぶり続ける見通し
― 高金利継続による利払い負担が財政を圧迫。財政を巡る不確実性が高い状況は続く見通し
― 欧州各国では財政懸念や政情不安を背景に高債務国中心に国債利回り上昇
― 財政への懸念を払拭しにくい状況が続くなか、英国でも金利急騰とこれに伴う財政悪化リスクには要注意
政府債務残高と10年債利回り変化幅の関係

中国：製造業利益は引き続きAI需要と資源高がけん引。7～9月期の利益率は前期を下回る
 9月の製造業総利益は前年比＋14.4％と前月（同＋9.1％）から加速。引き続きAI需要と資源高がけん引
― コンピューター・通信機器は同＋72.9％の増益。石油・石炭加工は単月赤字から一転し、同10倍超の伸び
― 他方、鉄鋼は単月赤字に転落したほか、自動車も同▲68.5％の減益。過剰生産業種は厳しい状況
 7～9月期の製造業の売上高利益率は5.0％と4～6月期（5.5％）から低下。自動車など幅広い業種で前期を下回る
― コンピューター・通信機器は7.4％で、前年（4.3％）から急速に改善。資源高で非鉄金属加工も好調

― 他方、自動車は2.5％と、前年（3.8％）から大幅に悪化。過当競争による販売価格の低下が響いた格好

― 韓国、台湾の主要な半導体・電子部品企業は、生産能力増強のため大規模な設備増強投資を計画
― 足元では韓国、台湾ともに半導体産業の機械設備投資が拡大傾向。主要各社による投資計画の進捗を示唆

 中東情勢緊迫化後、食料や燃料を中心にインフレ率が急上昇したタイとフィリピンでは、4～6月期に消費の伸びが減速

― タイでは燃料を中心とする交通費、フィリピンでは交通関連に加え、宿泊飲食なども抑制する形で消費者が節約行動
 背景に実質賃金の鈍化による購買力の低下
― 購買力低下を受け、タイは6月～11月に消費支援策（食品・日用品などの購入補助）を実施、フィリピンは政府による企
業への補助も伴いながら首都圏の最低賃金の大幅な引き上げを6月に発令。物価上昇への対応が両国の政治課題に

日本：基調的物価は2％到達も安定化には距離。日銀は利上げの影響を見極め微妙な舵取りへ
13
日銀10月会合のポイント（展望レポート・総裁会見）
（出所）日本銀行「経済・物価情勢の展望」より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
 日銀は基調的物価について「2％目標と概ね整合的な水準」に評価変更も、10月会合では利上げを見送り
― 今後は物価を2％で安定化させる局面へ。金融環境は緩和的との評価を維持し、次回12月会合で追加利上げの公算大
― 経済・物価はオントラックとの評価。原油価格の想定上振れを一部反映も見通し修正は軽微
― 総裁会見では「直近で物価上振れリスクが高まったわけではない」との発言。一段の利上げ加速は喫緊の必要性なし
 利上げを通じた基調的物価への影響を見極める手がかりとして、日銀は金融環境を注視

― 銀行・企業向けアンケート調査や銀行貸出残高にみられる変化、グローバル金融市場からの影響波及には要注意
経済・物価見通し
（注） 青字＝上昇修正、赤字＝下方修正。ドバイ原油価格は1バレル105ドル程度を出発
点に70ドル程度まで下落していくと前提。飲食料品消費税率の引き下げの影響は
織り込まず
（出所） 日本銀行より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
全体として修正は軽微。日銀見通し
対比でオントラックとの評価
展望レポート
• 基調的物価上昇率は2%物価目標と概ね整合的な水準
• 実質金利は上昇しているが、短期中心になお低水準。資金需要、
資産市場動向等も踏まえると金融環境は依然緩和した状態
• 名目賃金は前年並みのベースアップが実現するもとで、着実な増
加が続く見込み
• 海外の多くの中央銀行が利上げ方向に政策を転換。グローバルな
金融環境の変化がわが国の経済・物価に及ぼす影響に留意
会見中の総裁発言
• 物価上振れリスクはなおも無視できないが、1カ月前から物価上振
れリスクは大きく高まったわけではない

• 基調的物価が2%にアンカーされている度合いは極めて低い
• 金融政策が適時適切に発動され、緩和度合いを調整していくこと
で期待が安定化されていく
• 本支店ヒアリングでは、中小零細を中心に資金繰りがタイトになり
つつあるという声が聞かれ、今後の広がりを注意深くみていく
• 早めの政策対応を基本方針とするべき状況までは至っていない

日本：AI関連財の増産を背景に設備投資は今後拡大も、輸入依存の高さが成長の抑制要因に
14
 9月鉱工業生産は前月比＋2.2％と3カ月ぶりに増産。26年に入ってから増産基調を維持
― 増産の主因はAI関連。旺盛なAI関連需要を背景に、半導体製造装置などの増産が鉱工業生産をけん引
― 加えて、自動車生産の持ち直しなど、熊本地震や豪雨による下振れからの回復も生産の押し上げに寄与
 AI関連を中心に増産基調の生産動向は、今後の設備投資拡大を示唆
― ただし、資本財の輸入依存度は上昇傾向。AI関連業種で設備不足感が強まるなか、資本財の供給を輸入に頼る構造
― 人手不足などの供給制約で国内メーカーの増産が追いつかず、拡大する投資需要の一部が海外に流出の可能性も

（2026年10月2日発刊）

世界経済はどこへ向かうのか？中東危機やAI・レジリエンス関連の投資が
もたらす変化や、経済・金融市場の行方を展望
【ポイント①】
中東危機が⾧期化するなかでも、AI・レジリエンス関連の旺盛な投資需要に
支えられ、世界経済は3％台の成⾧ペースを維持
【ポイント②】
世界はインフレ・高金利の新常態へ。成⾧・負担の偏在が経済主体間の
「耐性格差」を拡大
【ポイント③】
海外主要中銀はインフレ警戒から利上げモードへ、日銀も物価上振れを警戒

## Document 082: 2026金融街论坛年会解读解读：筑牢流动性防线，提升制度包容性

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026金融街论坛年会解读解读：筑牢流动性防线，提升制度包容性.pdf
Report source: 国新证券股份
Date: 2026-11-01
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

市场研究部 2026年10月20日
事件
10 月 19 日，2026 年金融街论坛年会召开，央行、金融监管
总局、证监会、国家外汇管理局主要负责人分别发表演讲。
解读
一、央行行长潘功胜的主题演讲的主要政策
一是将会同金融监管总局、中国证监会设立应对非银机构大额
赎回的流动性支持工具。
二是扩大常备借贷便利（SLF）的适用机构范围。
三是推动银行间和交易所债券市场互联互通落地。
四是试点进一步便利境外金融机构在境内外汇市场开展人民币
购售业务。
五是会同科技部等 10 个部门出台《关于金融支持建设北京
（京津冀）、上海（长三角）、粤港澳大湾区国际科技创新中心的
指导意见》。
此外，今年 6月，人民银行设立了境外央行回购工具，支持境
外央行或货币当局、国际金融组织、主权财富基金，通过债券回购
方式从人民银行获取人民币流动性，便利其人民币流动性管理和人
民币资产配置。
今年初，人民银行平稳切换了数字人民币新计量框架，数字人
民币定位由现金切换为存款货币。
二、金融监管总局局长丁向群主题演讲的主要政策

切实提升监管有效性，有力防控金融风险。一是坚持风险导向
强化金融监管。二是适应实践需要健全金融法治。三是突出重点环
节提高监管能力。
加力推动改革转型，强化高质量发展金融支撑。一是不断优化
机构布局。二是大力规范市场秩序。三是持续提升金融服务适配性。
始终坚持互利共赢，持续推动金融高水平开放。一是积极拓展
金融开放新空间。二是有效服务国际经贸往来。三是推动完善全球
金融治理体系。
宏观研究
筑牢流动性防线，提升制度包容性
——2026金融街论坛年会解读解读
市场表现截至 2026.10.20
资料来源：国家统计局,国新证券整理。
分析师：郝大明
登记编码：S1490514010002
邮箱：haodaming@crsec.com.cn
证券研究报告

### Page 2

三、证监会主席吴清主题演讲的主要政策
第一，坚持稳字当头，全力维护资本市场安全稳定。
第二，突出守正创新，持续深化资本市场包容性改革。
第三，强化依法从严，大力培育规范健康的市场生态。
第四，深化务实合作，有序扩大市场高水平对外开放。
四、外管局局长朱鹤新主题演讲的主要政策
近期，在存量政策提质增效基础上，结合新形势新要求，以及
调研中发现的企业新的实际需求，拟集中推出一批增量措施，支持
企业更好参与国际合作与竞争，更好利用两个市场、两种资源。
一是推出经常项目便利化一揽子政策，更好满足外贸企业多样
化结算需求，支持贸易新业态新模式发展，促进外贸提质增效。
二是推出优化资本项目外汇管理一揽子政策，更好满足企业跨
境投融资和资金配置需求，服务“引进来”和“走出去”，拓展双
向投资合作空间。
三是推动外汇市场开放发展，扩大证券公司结售汇业务试点，
支持金融机构做好企业特别是中小企业汇率风险管理服务，提升跨
境服务能力，更好支持离在岸金融市场协同发展，助力人民币国际
化。
五、我们的解读
1、筑牢流动性防线，巩固完善稳市机制建设

维护市场稳定是资本市场健康发展的前提。人民银行行长潘功
胜宣布，将会同金融监管总局、证监会设立应对非银机构大额赎回
流动性支持工具，专门针对资管机构遭遇集中赎回、债券市场陷入
“赎回—抛售”负向循环的风险场景，通过高等级抵押品融资，切
断风险自我放大链条，补齐非银体系流动性应急保障短板，提升金
融体系抗冲击能力。这一安排与此前偏重银行体系的流动性管理形
成互补，将非银机构纳入宏观审慎管理视野，显著增强了金融体系
应对突发冲击的韧性。同时，央行也将同步推出扩大常备借贷便利
适用机构范围、推动交易所与银行间债券市场互联互通落地等配套
举措，打通跨市场资金流转渠道，优化货币市场利率调控框架，为
资本市场营造稳定适宜的货币环境。
证监会主席吴清释放了两个具有即时稳市场的信号。一是同意
首批 18只主动 ETF注册，明确将其定位为“推动基金产品创新发
展的重要举措，为市场引入更多增量资金”。在宽基 ETF 已成为
近期市场企稳重要力量的背景下，主动 ETF 的扩容意味着增量资
金入市渠道的进一步拓宽。二是强调“高度警惕境外挂钩 A股个股

的杠杆产品等新风险点”，强化外部风险输入的前置防范，体现了
监管层在开放环境下对新型风险的前瞻性关注。另外，在中长期稳
市机制方面，吴清主席明确提出要“推动进一步健全适配长期投资

### Page 3

的长周期考核机制，扩大中长期资金来源、渠道和入市方式，持续
深化公募基金改革，壮大市场稳定力量”。这意味着政策思路正从
短期的流动性干预，转向培育具有逆周期属性的制度性资本。当公
募基金、保险资金、养老金等真正以年度甚至跨年度维度评估投资
绩效时，市场的非理性波动才有望获得结构性的平抑力量。
2、强化银行保险业风险治理，夯实中长期资金根基
金融监管总局局长丁向群从机构端风险防控、行业改革、对外
开放角度，明确与资本市场改革相配套的监管导向。丁向群强调要
打好风险化解主动仗，稳妥处置重点领域风险，健全风险监测预警
与早期纠正机制，实现全部金融活动监管 “全覆盖、无例外”，
消除跨市场、跨业态监管盲区，守住不发生系统性风险底线。监管
持续强化银行保险机构的风险约束，引导保险资金等长期资金规范
参与资本市场投资，不能理解为中长期资金的收缩，而是旨在通过
净化机构生态，为资本市场投融资功能发挥筑牢中介资金基础，防
范机构风险向股票、债券市场传导溢出。另外，丁向群表示将支持
更多外资机构和长期资本来华展业兴业，鼓励外资机构发挥财富管

理、养老金融领域专业优势，有助于扩大中长期资金的来源，进一
步巩固资本市场发展的稳定性。
3、深化资本市场包容性改革，为新质生产力铺设制度通道
以资本市场服务科技创新，是本次论坛改革主线。吴清主席强
调未来将“落实落细‘两创板’改革举措”、“实施更具包容性适
应性的发行上市、并购重组等制度”、“择机发布新修订的上市公
司证券发行注册管理办法”，三项表述层层递进，核心是让那些处
于成长期、尚未盈利但具备核心技术能力的企业，能够更顺畅地获
得直接融资支持。这一监管信号与当前市场结构的变化形成了有意
味的呼应。当前科技板块尽管经历了剧烈调整，但其盈利预期并未
被系统性下调，电子行业近四周上调 2027年业绩预测的公司占比
仍居前列。调整消化的主要是前期过高的估值溢价与拥挤的交易结
构，而非产业逻辑本身。此时本次强调未来将出台更具包容性的制
度安排，实质上是在市场情绪的低位区间，为下一轮科技企业的资
本化提前铺设通道。同时，吴清主席提出“加快推动出台上市公司
监管条例”与“加力推进证券公司高质量发展行动”，则从公司治

理与中介机构两个层面，为包容性改革提供了质量约束与专业支撑，
避免包容性演变为降低标准的代名词。
4、强化投资端保护，修复市场信心锚点
信心的修复，离不开对中小投资者合法权益的实质性保障。吴
清主席在本次论坛上明确推出的四项近期举措，直指市场关切的痛
点。其中，完善违规减持处罚规则与制定发布证券市场先行赔付指
导意见尤为值得关注。前者通过细化认定标准、坚持过罚相当，遏
制利用信息优势与持股成本优势进行监管套利的行为，维护了二级
市场交易的公平性；后者则通过明确适用主体与实施程序，为受损

### Page 4

投资者提供了更为便捷高效的救济渠道。从市场反馈看，10 月 19
日上证指数收涨 0.39%，银行等低估值板块表现稳健，一定程度上
反映了政策信号对短期情绪的稳定作用。尽管单日涨幅不宜过度解
读，但监管层将“保护投资者”从原则宣示转化为可操作的制度安
排，其累积效应将逐步体现在市场风险溢价的回落之中。
5、统筹开放与安全，拓展资本市场境内外配置价值
在外部环境复杂性上升的背景下，资本市场的高水平开放并未
止步，而是呈现出更强调“可预期性”与“风险可控”的特征。国
家外汇管理局局长朱鹤新公布一批外汇领域增量改革措施，其中优
化资本项目外汇管理一揽子政策和扩大证券公司结售汇业务试点等
举措，能直接服务跨境投融资便利化，便利境外机构投资 A股，也
便利境内主体参与全球市场配置。吴清主席也提出提升 QFII 投资
运作便利度、支持境内外机构合作推出基于中国资产的指数产品，
并加快推进人民币股票交易柜台与 REITs纳入沪深港通。这些举措
的深层逻辑在于，通过降低跨境交易的制度性摩擦成本，吸引更多

以配置为导向的长期外资流入。
当前沪深 300 滚动市盈率处于历史中低位区间，全 A 市盈率
中位数较年中高点已有明显回落，中国资产在全球范围内的相对性
价比正在改善。开放举措的稳步推进，为全球投资者分享中国经济
结构转型的红利提供了更畅通的渠道，同时也在资本项下形成“有
管理的流入”格局，避免短期投机资本大进大出对市场稳定性造成
冲击。
综上，本次金融街论坛出台的政策组合，反映出监管对于未来
资本市场稳健发展的完整逻辑：以流动性支持工具守住不发生系统
性风险的底线，以包容性制度改革培育增量优质供给，以投资者保
护举措修复市场信任，以有序开放引导长期资本配置。相关政策并
非孤立的举措，而是多部门协同的系统安排。短期重在缓释市场冲
击、稳定市场预期，中长期重在完善资本市场基础制度，理顺投资
与融资的平衡关系。预计随着政策效力的逐步释放，资本市场投资
与融资相协调的功能将更为健全，其在服务“十五五”经济社会高
质量发展中的枢纽作用，也将获得更为坚实的制度支撑。
投资建议
10月 19日召开的金融街论坛年会上，央行、金融监管总局、

证监会、国家外汇管理局主要负责人集中发声，围绕流动性保障、
制度创新、风险防控、高水平开放打出政策“组合拳”，从货币环
境、机构治理、投融资改革、跨境资本管理等多个维度，为资本市
场稳预期、强韧性、促改革搭建完整政策框架，有效回应了市场关
切，为下一阶段资本市场稳健发展提供了清晰的政策支撑。
风险提示
1、地缘政治恶化超预期；2、欧美经济衰退超预期。

瑞昱 (2379 TT) 
財報與展望均優於預期

永豐觀點 
目前股價持穩於前季法說時點，而公司在財報與展望(包括毛利率、營業費用控管)
似乎均有好轉現象，尤其毛利率似乎出現谷底回溫 (優於研究部原預期的內外承
壓)，又公司在網通/車載等業務持續進展，股價應有上升空間。 
 投資評價與建議 
中立調升至買進：由於財報與展望均優於預期，毛利率谷底回溫，營業費用謹慎控
管，故研究部上修獲利預估值，預估瑞昱2026 年稅後 EPS 30.64 元(原預估 27.66
元)，2027 年稅後 EPS 35.19 元(原預估 29.15 元)。研究部認為瑞昱(1) 身為晶片
大廠，在資源調度、產品完整度、晶片優化或議價能力，相對小廠更具競爭力 (或
許也有機會提高市場份額)，此似乎也體現於 3Q26 財報與 4Q26 展望中，(2) 中
長期受會網通/車載/伺服器/邊緣 AI 裝置涉足而帶動營運成長，故將評等由中立調
升至買進建議，目標價 915 元(以 26X2027 年 EPS 計算)。

瑞昱企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。

瑞昱為網通晶片大廠 ：瑞昱為全球網通晶片大廠，若以公司營收排名為全球第八大

發科、聯詠等。 
3Q26 獲利優於預期：瑞昱 3Q26 營收 377.32 億元(+0.5%QoQ，+27.9% YoY)，
毛利率 47.4%，稅後獲利 41.26 億元(+8%QoQ，+20%YoY)，稅後 EPS 8 元，獲
利優於預期，主要是營收、毛利率與營業費用控管均較預期佳。瑞昱3Q26 財報細
項要點如下：(1) 營收季增 0.5%，優於研究部原預期的季減 3%，主要是網通業務
成長驅使，以及部分產品價格調漲，從而抵銷筆電/TV/遊戲機需求減緩的衝擊。若
究終端應用分拆，PC 因終端市場需求疲軟，瑞昱 3Q26 PC 比重由前季的 33%降
至 30%(3Q26 PC: 非 PC 比重為 30%:70%。(2) 毛利率由 2Q26 的 47%微幅增至
47.4%(優於研究部原預期將受製造成本上升而使毛利率承壓)，主要是產品結構變
化(高階產品比重增加 )，以及產品因應成本上揚而進行轉嫁。 (3) 營業費用季減
0.6%，反映公司在投資研發的同時，仍對營業費用謹慎控管。(4) 業外收益僅 6.06
億元(季減 3900 萬元)， 主要是包含金融資產評價損失 。(5) 瑞昱庫存天數雖由2Q26
的 107 天增至 3Q26 的 136 天，不過主要是供應鏈持續有不確定性，公司為確保
客戶供貨的連貫性，故多建置庫存因應。 
4Q26 營收降幅不會如同過往第四季顯著：瑞昱認為(1) 4Q26 營運審慎穩健，雖
第四季通常為營運淡季， 但隨產品結構往高階挪移， 又價格為反映製造成本上升而
仍有調漲事宜，故營收降幅較為平緩，其中PC 需求仍相對疲弱。(3) 2H26 毛利率
將逐步回溫。整體而言，研究處預估瑞昱 4Q26 營收 348.5 億元(-7.6%QoQ，
+32.6%YoY)，毛利率隨產品結構變化與轉嫁而略增至 47.6%，稅後獲利 35.34 億
元(-14%QoQ，+33%YoY)，稅後 EPS 6.86 元。

### Page 3

3

2027 年營運應仍能維持成長力道：研究部認為台系 IC 設計族群今明年將持續遭
遇內外交雜的挑戰(內有製造成本攀升、外有消費電子需求疲軟)，不過瑞昱身為晶
片大廠，在資源調度、產品優化與議價能力均相對同業更具競爭力，使得營運表現
應能優於產業狀態(其實從瑞昱財報亦能得證，公司即使 30%營收與 PC 相關，但
其 3Q26 獲利數字與 4Q26 展望均仍穩健)。研究部認為瑞昱動能來自下述因子驅
動：(1) 網通隨電信業者對 25GPON 架構進行結構性測試、乙太網路交換器受益
於硬體升級、消費者與中小企業路由器往Ｍesh/三頻配置/WiFi7 升級，並且企業
級網路管理需求與管理型交換器持續普及中，均將推升瑞昱網通業務長期穩健成
長。(2) WiFi7 滲透率提升。隨瑞昱客戶的新開案均基於 WiFi7 作設計，瑞昱預期
WiFi7 滲透率將由 2026 年的 30%攀升至 40%。WiFi 聯盟計畫 WiFi8 將於 06/2027
完成認證測試，並將於 09/2027 前正式啟動 WiFi8 認證，瑞昱預期 2H27 公司
WiFI8 產品可望步入量產，以利客戶搭載 WiFi8 產品更廣泛於 2028 年上市。(3) 
光通訊專注 112G/224G SerDes 開發(4nm 製程)，100G 光模組控制晶片將以雲
端、資料中心與電信網路為主要著墨項目，明年量產規模提升，並且 400G/800G 
PAM4 DSP預計 2H27 推出。(4) 電動車成長持續帶動瑞昱車載乙太網路進展，瑞
昱預期新車搭載乙太網路的滲透率已超過 50%。(5) 中長期邊緣 AI 業務(包括車載
/機器人/IOT)與高效能運算 (比如瑞昱導入乙太網路 /交換器/xPON/SSD/IO 橋接
器/USB hub/光通訊)，目標 2030 年營收比重達 30%。 
中立轉買進：由於財報與展望均優於預期，毛利率谷底回溫，營業費用謹慎控管，
故研究部上修獲利預估值，預估瑞昱2026 年營收 1466 億元(+19%YoY)，毛利率
47.91%，稅後獲利 157.9 億元(+7%YoY)，稅後 EPS 30.64 元(原預估 27.66 元)，
並預估 2027 年營收 1594 億元(+9%YoY)，毛利率 48.37%，稅後獲利 181.4 億
元(+15%YoY)，稅後 EPS 35.19 元(原預估 29.15 元)。研究部認為瑞昱(1) 身為晶
片大廠，在資源調度、產品完整度、晶片優化或議價能力，相對小廠更具競爭力(或
許也有機會提高市場份額)，此似乎也體現於 3Q26 財報與 4Q26 展望中，(2) 中
長期受會網通/車載/伺服器/邊緣 AI 裝置涉足而帶動營運成長，故將評等由中立調
升至買進建議，目標價 915 元(以 26X2027 年 EPS 計算)。

## Document 100: 261025_gs_TW-PCB-CCL

Source PDF: all report pdf/261025_gs_TW-PCB-CCL.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 17
Extraction status: ok

### Page 2

PCB CCL upgrading continues: M10 is under qualification, which should start 
shipments from 2H27, while we expect M11 shipments to start no later than 2030  
For the PCB CCL industry overall, the key revenue/profit driver in our view is the solid 
specification upgrading trend (e.g., AI M7 ramp up in 2024, the M8 ramp in 2025, and 
M9 in 2026, Exhibit 3) which continues to drive high-end CCL players’ ASP growth by 
25-50% YoY annually in 2024-2026, and we are seeing the trend continuing and 
accelerating in coming years, despite the concerns of some investors in our 
conversations that a technology migration slowdown is impacting CCL players’ 
revenue/profit growth opportunity.  
During the TPCA show, we saw both EMC and TUC launch their M10/M11 solutions 
at the event, while both companies mentioned they are closely engaging with end 
customers for the next-generation AI R&D projects, based on the M10/11 solution (+ 
under qualification now). We believe EMC should start shipping its M10 products from 
2H27 at the earliest for specific AI customer’s GPU AI rack solutions and that shipments 
should ramp up significantly in 2028 as more GPU and ASIC AI projects start adopting 
this solution. For TUC’s M10 products, we believe the company will start shipment 
from 2028 for not only high-end 3.2T Ethernet switch projects but also for some ASIC 
projects.  
Both EMC and TUC are currently under verification with clients for their M10/M11 grade 
CCL, which incorporates Q-glass. In terms of the possibility of adopting PTFE-based 
CCL, the new solution continues to face production difficulties, making us less 
positive on the solution’s progress, and we only expect the MP (mass production) 
time to occur after 2H28 at the earliest (our supply chain checks suggest the 
production yield for PTFE-based PCB is running at 10-15% vs. average yield of 
80-90% for PCB with M9 grade CCL adopting Q-glass). As a result, we believe the PCB 
upgrade for switch tray in Nvidia’s Rubin Ultra GPU servers starting from 2H27 could 
have a high likelihood of adopting M9/M10 grade CCL with Q-glass/HC (Hydrocarbon) 
instead of PTFE, potentially implying better EMC/TUC share than we currently expect 
over the next 2-3 years).

to go down with customers’ investment in equipment or

### Page 3

New opportunity to enter the high ASP/GM ABF substrate supply chain for TW CCL 
supply chain players 
Given the relatively slow capacity investment to date from Japanese substrate material 
suppliers (ABF CCL/ T-glass/ micro thin copper foil etc.industries are mostly dominated 
by Japanese players, with more than 95% market share in the past 10+ years) while 
demand continues to trend up (we are seeing ABF substrates’ material usage doubling 
every 1-2 years, due to the expanding substrate size/ increasing substrate layer counts / 
decreasing substrate production yield rate), we started to see end customers 
qualification processes for Taiwan and mainland China ABF substrate supply chain 
vendors accelerate from 1H26, to better overcome supply constraints, implying a 
long term share gain trend from the Taiwan/mainland China new substrate material 
suppliers (including EMC and Co-Tech in our coverage space). We therefore believe 
EMC likely starts taking ABF substrate CCL market share from 2H26 (TAM could be 
up from US$0.8bn-1.2bn in 2026 to US$2.0bn-2.5bn in 2027 and US$4.0bn-4.5bn in

### Page 4

2028, accounting for ~15%/19%/22% of EMC’s 2026/27/28E revenue, per GSe, 
which not only suggests significant upside potential for EMC’s ASP outlook (ABF 
substrate CCL ASP per full sheet is ~US$1,500 vs. M7/8 only at US$130/180, Exhibit 
5) but should also lead to a solid GM uptrend. We estimate the ABF substrate CCL GM is 
now at 50%+ which could further increase closer to the industry average of ~70%+ 
in the long term based on our industry checks (vs. our 2026/27/28 GM estimate on 
EMC is 35.2%/40.7%/43.4%). Also, we believe that micro-thin copper foil, which is 
dominated by Japan vendors, should also start to see some new suppliers starting from 
late 2027/early 2028, while Co-Tech said that it is preparing R&D resources and is 
working on designs for the new production lines for this new substrate-level product, 
which we estimate could start contributing to the company’s revenue from 2H28 at the 
earliest per GSe.  
Moreover, considering that the ABF substrate size/layer count both continue to increase, 
with less favorable yield rate and production efficiency, we expect the high-end AI ABF 
substrates consumption on ABF CCL to increase significantly. Based on prevailing 
specifications, we believe a quarter sheet of ABF CCL can currently produce ~6-8 units 
of high-end ABF substrates, but that number could decrease significantly to 2-3 units 
per quarter sheet in 2028 based on the substrate design, which should lead to a solid 
unit volume demand uptrend.  
In terms of supply, EMC said that it could have 1.5mn sheets/month capacity by end of 
2027 for ABF substrate CCL during TPCA event, which would then likely account for 50% 
of the global ABF substrate CCL capacity on our estimates, suggesting a strong focus by 
the company to compete in this high ASP/GM product segment.

PCB/CCL upgrades to continue with limited risk from CPO adoption 
We see limited near term risk of Co-Packaged Optics (CPO) adoption as the industry 
should start with switch trays in GPU servers from 2028E, when we estimate switch 
board to account for 14% of a total CCL TAM of US$48bn in 2028E. In considering an 
extreme case where all switch boards are downgraded to M4/M7 grade CCL, we would 
expect the impact to result in a <10% reduction in the 2028E CCL TAM, an impact which 
we believe would be manageable. At the TPCA show, we saw ZDT showcase its 30 layers

### Page 5

HDI PCB switch board, and the company said that it is confident that switch board will
continue to see upgrades in terms of layer count and design complexity as optical
transceivers are being used as the mainstream solution for scale out, which requires high
end PCB/CCL for its longer electrical path comparing to CPO (Exhibit 7). ZDT mentioned
the possibility of scale up adopting CPO, but is unsure on the MP timing at this point, and
we see this technology as unlikely to be available before 2030E given its technology

complexities.
ZDT also showcased multiple optical module mSAP solutions, including
800G/1.6T/NPO/XPO, and we believe that optical transceivers, which rely on high end
PCB/CCL in switch trays, will remain mainstream in the foreseeable future and thus is
beneficial to the optical module mSAP suppliers (we expect optical module mSAP to see
87% 2026-28E CAGR, Exhibit 8). We believe ZDT is well-positioned to gain share in this
fast growing market as we believe the company is proactively expanding mSAP capacity

(we expect ZDT’s mSAP capacity to reach 5x in the next 2 years, see here, as well as the
company currently holds an industry-leading yield of 90%+ for its optical module mSAP
products. Optical module mSAP GMs currently average 40%-45%, and could potentially
reach 50%+ following pricing hikes and yield improvements, which would be beneficial
to ZDT’s profitability (our 2026/27/28 GM estimate on ZDT only at
24.7%/27.6%/31.4%).

EMC to see solid upside on profit potential with the new substrate CCL opportunity
We continue to see EMC make progress in the new ABF substrate CCL business, a market
which the company entered in early 2026 and started producing high-end ABF substrate
CCL from 2H26, with monthly revenues reaching NT$150-200mn (<1% of the company’s
Sep monthly revenue) for the most high-end TPU substrate CCL market (see here). We
believe EMC could become one of the market leaders within the next 3-5 years (we are

expecting 20-30% market share in 2028E and trending up significantly afterward), given
its track record of successfully entering new markets over the past two decades.
Furthermore, the company is well-positioned to gain share in the tight supply market as
the company is proactive with its substrate CCL expansion plans, and the company
expects to expand its substrate CCL capacity to 4-5x of current level by 2027 (current
substrate CCL capacity at 300k sheet/month), which should account for 50% of the

global substrate CCL capacity by end of 2027E, implying a good opportunity for the
company to further ramp up market share in the long term.
M10/11 upgrades still the key to earnings growth for EMC & TUC in the next 3+
years, while the favorable S/D outlook should continue to drive the like-for-like
base pricing in coming quarters
We see EMC and TUC being well-positioned in the ongoing CCL upgrade trend as both
companies are currently under verification with clients for their respective next

generation M10/M11 grade CCL and are also future proofing their product-portfolios by
continuing to develop PTFE-based CCL. For the new M10 grade CCL, we expect EMC will
start shipments from 2Q27-3Q27 while TUC should start shipments from 2028, leading
to meaningful upside potential for each company’s revenue, as EMC/TUC’s current ASPs
have been approximately US$63/46 in 2026 while the M10 grade CCL ASP is currently
priced at ~US$800 per sheet, implying substantial ASP upside potential from here, all

else equal.
We continue to see a solid S/D outlook for high-end CCLs in coming years (see here) as
capacity expansion for these CCLs continues to track slower than demand growth (see
here). However, we note TUC revised up its capacity target and expects it to increase
2.5x+ over 2026-30 (8/5/30% upward revision to the year-end target capacity for
2026/27/28 from previous guidance), and based on company comments we believe the
new capacity is fully booked by clients, implying a solid revenue/profit growth outlook.

Co-Tech is currently obtaining LTAs to secure high-end copper foil capacity,
suggesting a solid long term market share and revenue outlook
We believe Co-Tech is well-positioned to benefit from the structural demand for high
end copper foil in a shortage environment, as the company announced capacity
expansion plans for a new Plant 4 in Malaysia, with capacity being secured by more than
one client, per the company. The company now target its annual HVLP3+ effective

capacity to reach 10K/20K/30K tons by end of 2027/28/29 (equivalent of
833/1666/2500 tons/month by 2027/28/29 end vs. current HVLP3+ effective capacity
of ~250 tons/month). This should enable the company to achieve further gains in market
share amid high end copper foil supply shortage environment (see here), as well as
seeing a better pricing environment going forward.
We expect Co-Tech’s new capacity, after ramp up, should continue to see 100% UTR, as

25 October 2026   7
Goldman Sachs Taiwan TechnologyFor the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 8

most of the new capacity has been booked/sponsored by end customers already, 
implying a solid market share and revenue outlook in coming years. Moreover, we believe 
the company will start to recognize customer subsidies from 2027 at the earliest, 
implying further incremental upside to profitability in coming years.  
ZDT continues to maintain key one-stop shop status for all ABF/BT/HDI/HLC/mSAP 
products, suggesting solid market share outlook in long term 
We remain positive on ZDT as the company continues to make progress with its optical 
module mSAP and AI PCB business, and the company mentioned that both of these 
business are seeing capacity bookings by clients. We believe ZDT will become a market 
share leader in the optical module mSAP industry (see here), as we expect the company 
to expand mSAP capacity by 5x over the next two years based on announced capex 
plans. Most suppliers in the optical module mSAP industry are proactively expanding 
capacity as CPO adoption risk remains limited.  
Also, considering that ZDT is the only company that manufactures all types of 
ABF/BT/HDI/HLC/mSAP products, it is well positioned for further share gains not only in 
the optical transceiver market, but also in the AI PCB, ABF, BT markets. Additionally, we 
estimate that these new products will likely carry 35-50%+ GMs in 2027, implying a solid 
revenue/profit outlook for the company going forward.  
We also see other companies within the CCL/IC substrate sector flagging supply 
tightness across the whole supply chain as industry expansion has not been sufficient to 
meet prevailing demand. This is in line with our view that suppliers with timely capacity 
expansion plans are better positioned to make further market share gains and benefit 
from stronger pricing/profitability amid a supply-constrained environment.

Investment Thesis - Elite Material  
EMC, a key supplier of high-end HDI material (70%+ market share) and SLP material 
(90%+ market share), has been focusing on the high-speed switch/server CCL market for 
3-5+ years. We are positive on its market share trajectory in the server industry and 
expect EMC to increase its share from <10% in the Purley generation to 15-20%/20+% 
in the Whitley/Eagle Stream platforms. EMC is also proactively expanding its share in the 
switch market (40%/30%+ in 2024/23 vs. <5% before 2019), with an expanding AI 
customer base (only one 400G customer in 3Q20, to being one of the largest suppliers in 
2023), which should continue to benefit the company’s top/bottom line growth over 
time. We believe EMC’s leading position in the AI server CCL market should drive strong 
revenue growth and GM/OPM expansion in the long term, given that it is the major 
supplier for all Nvidia/Google/AWS AI server projects, and we expect it to maintain its 
leadership position. Considering its leading position in the high-end CCL industry, solid 
production skills and earnings growth outlook, we are Buy rated on the stock. With the 
shares trading at a 2026E P/E below AI server component suppliers and supported by a 
solid growth outlook, we view valuation as attractive. 
Key downside risks include: (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) 
rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and 
(3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Elite Material  
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$10,200 is based on 27x 2H27-1H28E P/E 
(in line with the peak P/E multiple for AI component suppliers in the past 3 years).  
Key downside risks:  (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) rising 
trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and (3) 
rising competition from mainland China peers.

Investment Thesis - Taiwan Union Technology Corp. 
TUC is a key high-end global CCL supplier that focuses on M7+ grade high-speed CCL 
(key applications include high-end switches (100G+) and AI server materials), with 20%+ 
market share in the past two years. The company continues to expect its unit market 
share in the server industry to increase from 15% in Whitley and Purley processors to 
higher in the Eagle Stream generation, and plans to launch two types of products for 
low-end and high-end customers (T2A and T2C) to gain market share. Also, strong 
growth in 400G/800G switch shipments and new 800G switch shipments, scheduled for 
launch in 2025-27, should continue to be a key demand driver for TUC, in our view. 
Additionally, the company is working on high-end AI projects with CSP and enterprise 
players, which we believe should drive revenue momentum in the long term. We view the 
stock as undervalued vs. Taiwan CCL peers on a P/E basis and rate it Buy.  
Key downside risks include: (1) slower-than-expected share gains in the low-loss CCL 
segment; (2) rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker server and switch 
shipments globally; and (3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Taiwan Union Technology Corp. 
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$3,010 is based on a 22x 2027E P/E (+2x 
STDV higher than the past 3-year industry average P/E multiple).  
Key downside risks:  (1) slower-than-expected share gains in the low-loss CCL segment; 
(2) rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker server and switch shipments 
globally; and (3) rising competition from mainland China peers. 
Investment Thesis - Co-Tech 
Co-Tech is a global leading general server copper foil supplier, with ~60% market share 
in 2025 per GSe. We expect the company to enjoy solid share gain opportunities in the 
near term, considering its proactive HVLP capacity expansion plan and strong 
know-how in the advanced copper foil industry. Also, adoption of HVLP4 copper foil 
from AI servers and 1.6T networking switches starting in 2H26 should be a key demand 
driver for Co-Tech, in our view. Additionally, the company’s proactive pricing strategy 
and ability to improve production yield should drive revenue momentum in the long 
term.  
We are Buy rated as we believe Co-Tech will see better revenue/profitability in the long 
term given its shift to a more premium product mix along with proactive capacity plans 
(Plant 3 to be ready by end of 2027) and this should lead to strengthened valuations.

Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$940 is based on a 22x 2028E P/E, which is 
+1SD above the company’s average upcycle valuation in the past 10-years. 
Key downside risks: (1) earlier than expected CPO adoption in the computing IC, (2) 
slower than expected yield rate improvement speed, and (3) much stronger than 
expected capacity growth from HVLP peers, limiting pricing power

### Page 12

Investment Thesis - Zhen Ding Technology
Zhen Ding Technology (ZDT) is the global leading PCB supplier (top 1 in market share in
sales) with 7-10% market share, and started its ABF substrate business from 2023 in
Shenzhen, China; it has the highest ABF revenue exposure to the China market compared
to all other ABF substrate suppliers. We believe ZDT will hold more than 40% ABF
substrate market share in China by 2027E (up from 31% in 2024) and benefit the most

from the strong and growing China ABF substrate market (53% 2025-27E CAGR) due to
the increasing in-sourcing ratio for the AI computing ASIC/GPU market in China. We also
expect the increasing foldable smartphone penetration rate in the long term to drive
ZDT’s FPC TAM, and account for 39% of the company’s total revenue in 2027E. We see
further revenue upside potential from foldable phones. We are Buy rated mainly due to
our positive view on the company’s China ABF substrate business opportunity, which

should not only drive ZDT’s revenue growth, but also improve its OPM level in the long
term. We see upside for the stock price considering the company’s solid growth
opportunity, and our TP implied P/E multiple is still lower than the company’s past 10+
year P/E average, which we believe is fair considering the potential upside from China
ABF substrate market and increasing foldable phone penetration rate.
Valuation: Our 12-month TP of NT$1,025 is based on a SOTP valuation methodology,

including (1) 3.5x blended 2028E P/B for ABF & BT substrate, (2) 20x 2028E P/E for
high-growth AI server/switch related PCB (in line with industry average), and (3) 8x
2028E P/E for low-growth consumer electronics PCB (in line with industry downcycle
average valuation).
Key downside risks include: (1) weaker-than-expected iPhone demand or slower
content upgrade, (2) delay in new substrate capacity or slower-than-expected yield rate
ramp up progress, and (3) fiercer competition in China ABF market.

anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

## Document 124: 261027_ubs_powertech

### Page 1

ab 27 October 2026
Global Research
First Read
Powertech Technology
Subsidiaries drive stronger revenue/OP growth;
FOPLP expansion back on track
Q326 results broadly in-line despite better GM
Q326 EPS of NT$3.54 was broadly in-line with UBSe/consensus as stronger  revenue and
better GM were offset by higher tax and minority interests. Q326 sales rose 9.6% QoQ
to NT$25.3 bn on robust DRAM/eSSD demand and price hikes, ahead of UBSe/Street's
+5-6% QoQ.  GM expanded by 2.2ppt QoQ to 24.0%, supported by better pricing,

higher loadings and more favourable  mix. By business, higher GM testing  increased by
1ppt QoQ to 24% of total sales, while lower margin SiP/module declined 2ppt QoQ to
9%, and packaging  increased 1ppt QoQ to 67%. By entity, we estimate 43%-owned
Greatek  and 61%-owned Teraprobe  each delivered mid-teens % QoQ revenue growth
in Q326, versus  only mid-single % growth at PTI standalone. The stronger contribution
from subsidiaries, however, resulted in higher effective tax rate and minority interests.

Near-term outlook  remains constructive
Management remains constructive on the near-term outlook, supported by AI/cloud
investment cycle, continued memory supply constraint, and PTI's ongoing diversification
into non-memory segments. Q426F UTRs are expected to remain elevated following
over-90% levels in Q3, while ASPs should continue to increase as customers absorb
higher raw material costs. PTI is also expanding its exposure to logic Flip-Chip BGA, while

premium-segment automotive demand remains stable. Nevertheless, consumer
electronics demand is showing some weakness amid higher memory pricing. We
forecast Q426 sales  to grow 4% QoQ and  GM to expand  0.8ppt QoQ to 24.8%.
Looking into 2027E, we believe  GM to remain in the mid-20% range, as continued
memory supply tightness and  better mix should largely offset higher  depreciation.
FOPLP expansion back on track
PTI indicated its FOPLP expansion remains on track for mass production in 2027, with the

equipment delivery schedule having improved. It sees robust customer demand  with
notable increases in New Product Introduction (NPI). We continue to model FOPLP
capacity of 3K substrates/month in 2026E, rising to 6K by mid-2027E in Taiwan, with
panel substrate size of 510 mm x 515 mm. Importantly, PTI  aims to serve end-customers
directly and capture greater value-add, rather than operate  as an outsourcing partner to
leading wafer foundry. Its JV with Broadcom remains  under review by Taiwan's MOEA

Investment Commission with management expecting a decision in the  near-term.
Valuation: Raise PT to NT$280, retain NEUTRAL
We revise our 2026/27/28E EPS by 3%/9%/7% on better sales/GM, offset by higher tax
and minority interest from subsidiaries. We raise PT to NT$280 (from NT$260) with the
same 17x 2027E PE. While near-term outlook has improved we believe further upside
will depend on the execution of the  FOPLP ramp and the sustainability of pricing

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

3%/ 2%/ 1% in 2026-29E mainly on higher revenues of SiPh CW lasers, supported by 
strong client demand and capacity expansion. We revise up GM by 1ppts in 2026E on 
mix upgrade, and revise down Opex ratio in 2026-29E to reflect improving operational 
efficiency amid the larger scale.

Valuation: We employ a discounted P/E methodology (unchanged) and apply a 61.5x 
(unchanged, report link) target P/E multiple on our 2029E EPS, discounted back to 
2027E at a COE of 10.5% (beta 1.7x, risk free rate 1.6%, and market risk premium at 
5.1% - unchanged). Our target P/E multiple of 61.5x is derived from the PEG&M ratio of 
peers (unchanged) in the photonics supply chain. Consequently, our 12-month target 
price is now NT$4,490 (vs. previously at NT$4,459). Our new TP implied 2028E P/E of 
75x is between company’s avg. P/E at 62x and avg. +1 P/E at 91x. Maintain Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - LandMark 
Price Target Risks and Methodology - LandMark Optoelectronics Corp.  
Valuation: We employ a discounted P/E methodology, applying a 61.5x target P/E 
multiple on our 2029E EPS, then discount it back to 2027E at a COE of 10.5% (beta 1.7x, 
risk free rate 1.6% and market risk premium at 5.1%), to derive our 12-month target 
price of NT$4,490. Our target P/E multiple is based on the same PEG&M ratio as peers in 
the photonics supply chain. We are Buy-rated on LandMark. 
Key risk: Slower-than-expected SiPh penetration; stronger-than-expected competition; 
Integrated Device Manufacturers (IDMs) offering Epitaxial (EPI) wafers in-house.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

長榮航太 (2645 TT)  Trading Buy

預期波音 737 系列交機速度將略為放緩。  
投資建議 ：雖然 2026 年長榮航太的維修業務持續擴張，但考量
波音將維持 737 系列月均交機一段時間，可能會影響長榮航太
的製造部門復甦情形，故給予長榮航太 Trading Buy 的投資建
議。  
波音 737 系列月交機將維持一段時間，調整長榮航太後續製造
的營運預估： 波音於 10/27/2026 公告 3Q26 財報，並於法說會
上提及 :「當前需維持 737 系列月均交機量穩定在 47 架一段時
間，後續管理階層預期交機提升可能要到 2H27 才會上升至 52
架」，使得長榮航太的製造部門復甦仍需等待一段時間，故研
究部一併調整長榮航太後續的營運預估。  
3Q26 先前的維修漲價陸續貢獻，營運維持高檔： 3Q26 長榮航
太自 2H24 起陸續針對維修業務進行漲價後，加上全球機隊持
續供不應求，增加維修機率與維修產值，惟製造部門雖然波音
已將 737 系列交機上升至 47 架 /月，但短期拉貨力道仍有限，
使得長榮航太的製造業務與轉投資公司營運維持低檔，營運仍
以維修業務為主， 3Q26 營收 52.75 億元， QoQ+1.02% ，預估稅
後純益 8.31 億元， QoQ+10.34% ， EPS 2.22 元。  
2027 年空中巴士首件交付、無人機新訂單，有望成為中長期營
運新動能： 2027 年長榮航太除了持續受惠維修業務與波音交機
的逐年擴張之外，在新訂單的部分，先前與空中巴士的新訂單，
預估 1Q27 將迎來首件交付，並待後續取得特殊製程認證後，
2H27 有望迎來新訂單的量產，以及無人機訂單也有望成為長榮
航太新一波的營運動能，預估 2027 年營收 224.86 億元，
YoY+7.85% ，稅後純益 37.61 億元， YoY+17.06% ，EPS 10.04 元。

波音 737 系列月交機將維持一段時間，調整長榮航太後續製造的營運預估：
波音於 10/27/2026 公告 3Q26 財報，並於法說會上提及 :「當前需維持 737 系
列月均交機量穩定在 47 架一段時間，後續管理階層預期交機提升可能要到

間，故研究部一併調整長榮航太後續的營運預估。  
2027 年空中巴士首件交付、無人機新訂單，有望成為中長期營運新動能：  
2027 年長榮航太除了持續受惠維修業務與波音交機的逐年擴張之外，在新
訂單的部分，先前與空中巴士的新訂單，預估 1Q27 將迎來首件交付，並待
後續取得特殊製程認證後， 2H27 有望迎來新訂單的量產，以及無人機訂單
也有望成為長榮航太新一波的營運動能，預估 2027 年營收 224.86 億元，
YoY+7.85% ，稅後純益 37.61 億元， YoY+17.06% ， EPS 10.04 元。  
投資建議：  
雖然 2026 年長榮航太的維修業務持續擴張，但考量波音將維持 737 系列月
均交機一段時間，可能會影響長榮航太的製造部門復甦情形，故給予長榮航
太 Trading Buy 的投資建議。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

陳泳如  
kate401124-71795@esunsec.com.tw

研 究  報 告                
力積電(6770.TT) 
產業：電子–半導體 
記憶體漲價貢獻 4Q26 獲利維持高檔，規劃 3D AI Foundry 新廠  
 投資評價與建議 
3Q26獲利優於預期，且4Q26毛利率有望重回50%以上之高峰，加上明年記憶
體、成熟製程市況展望正向，故我們給予2.3倍PB，持續看好後續營運動能。 
 重點評論分析 
看好4Q26毛利率重回過往50%以上高峰： 基於 ： (1)IPC 、 網通和醫療等應用支
撐力積電DRAM代工需求，Flash業務中，SLC NAND則因記憶體大廠先進製
程產能排擠，獲得車用、網通等小容量應用降規需求挹注；(2) AI伺服器持續
支撐PMIC、分離式元件需求，另考量中介層與Wafer on Wafer於新竹廠區陸
續復線，將有利於3D AI Foundry營收動能回溫。預估有望季增18.3%至252.4
億元，毛利率53.6%，稅後EPS 1.86元。  
正向看待明年展望： 展望明年 ， 我們認為 ： (1)AI需求維持強勁且應用持續擴大 ，
預期記憶體供不應求之情況將至少延續至 2027年，其中市場預估明年DRAM
報價將延續漲勢；(2)邏輯業務中，PMIC、分離式元件等8吋產能持續受惠AI強
勁需求，我們預期明年台系成熟製程廠商產能利用率有望達90%以上，公司規
劃明年漲價約10~20%且產能將以LTA客戶為優先；(3)考量目前無塵室空間幾
乎用罄，公司將開始評估新廠並著重3D AI Foundry產能擴產，但計畫透過設
備租賃、客戶給付預付款形式由客戶參與部分投資金額，以此控制公司資本支
出。我們初步預估明年營收達1,241.8億元，年增60.3%，稅後EPS 10.65元。 
 投資風險 
記憶體報價下滑、3D AI Foundry進展不如預期。 
主要財務數據及預估值 
(單位:百萬元) 2022 2023 2024 2025 2026 2027 
營業收入 76,087 44,023 44,726 46,730 77,447 124,181 
營業毛利 35,579 5,292 525 -1,537 29,966 70,450

| 營運概況  
3Q26獲利優於預期 ：力積電公告3Q26自結營收213.4億元 ， 季增23.4% 、 年增80.3% ， 毛利率47.6% ， 稅後EPS 1.38
元，獲利表現優於預期，因毛利率受惠於平均ASP季增24%、產能利用率由2Q26的87%提升至3Q26的97%。 
4Q26營收雙位數成⻑，看好毛利率重回過往50%以上高峰：展望4Q26，基於：(1)記憶體大廠優先投資HBM並逐
步退出利基型DRAM市場，加上IPC、網通和醫療等應用支撐，使得力積電DRAM代工需求維持穩健，至於與美光
合作之1P製程進展順利，預計2Q27開始試產，Flash業務中，SLC NAND則因記憶體大廠先進製程產能排擠，獲得
車用、網通等小容量應用降規需求挹注，另公司維持MLC NAND今年底tape out之進度；(2)邏輯業務中，儘管消
費性電子需求不佳，但AI伺服器需求強勁持續支撐PMIC、分離式元件需求，其中公司PMIC代工服務已從0.15微米
切入55奈米並開始出貨，且獲客戶三年綁約，反映需求強勁，另考量中介層與Wafer on Wafer於新竹廠區陸續復
線，將有利於4Q26 AI Foundry營收動能回溫。綜上所述，公司估本季營收有望雙位數季增，我們則預估有望季增
18.3%至252.4億元，毛利率53.6%，回到過往50%以上之高峰水準，稅後EPS 1.86元。  
正向看待明年展望：展望明年，我們認為：(1)AI需求維持強勁且應用持續擴大，預期記憶體大廠優先投資先進製程
之策略不變，故認為整體記憶體市場供不應求之情況將至少延續至2027年，其中市場預估明年DRAM報價將延續
漲勢(但漲幅收斂)，隱含公司代工價格具上漲動能；(2)邏輯業務中，半導體庫存維持健康，且PMIC、分離式元件等
8吋產能持續受惠AI強勁需求(其中分離式元件80%產能已簽訂LTA) ， 我們預期明年台系成熟製程廠商產能利用率有
望達90%以上，基於產能吃緊將有利於明年成熟製程廠商議價能力，公司規劃明年漲價約10~20%且產能將以LTA
客戶為優先；(3)3D AI Foundry業務仍為未來投資主軸，且產能規劃與進展大致如期，唯⼀有較新進展之業務則為
Wafer on Wafer規劃1H27量產 ， 另公司考量目前無塵室空間幾乎用罄 ， 公司將開始評估新廠並著重3D AI Foundry
產能擴產，但計畫透過設備租賃、客戶給付預付款形式由客戶參與30~60%的投資金額，以此控制公司資本支出。
我們初步預估明年營收達1,241.8億元，年增60.3%，稅後EPS 10.65元。

## Document 225: [000270] 기아(000270) 잠시 쉬어갑니다기아(000270) 잠시 쉬어갑니다잠시 쉬어갑니다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000270] 기아(000270) 잠시 쉬어갑니다기아(000270) 잠시 쉬어갑니다잠시 쉬어갑니다 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

기아 (000270)

EARNINGS REVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.11.02  
잠시 쉬어갑니다

3Q26 Review 
기아는 3Q26P 매출액 31.6조원(+10.1% YoY, -4.4% QoQ), 영업이익 1.8조원
(+24.4% YoY, -30.8% QoQ, OPM 5.8%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회했다. 영업이익 증
감 요인은 환율 효과(+5,290억원), 물량 효과(+4,420억원), 믹스 효과(-4,650억원), 
인센티브/가격 효과(-7,270억원), 재료비 영향(-3,680억원), 기타(+4,130억원)이다. 
판매 성장에도 높아진 수익성 부담 
글로벌 도매 판매 84.7만대(+7.9% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 83.1만대
(+8.8%)를 기록했다. 미국 HEV와 서유럽 EV 판매가 각각 118%, 163% 증가하며 
글로벌 점유율은 4.0%까지 확대됐다. 다만, 판매 성장에도 원화 강세와 원자재 가격 
상승, EV 비중 확대에 따른 믹스 부담으로 수익성은 전분기 대비 하락했다. 유럽 내 
EV 판매가 늘어나면서 믹스가 악화되었으며, 전쟁으로 인한 원자재 상승으로 3분기 
재료비 부담은 전년 대비 약 3,700억 증가했다.  
연간 판매목표 335만대는 유지했으나, 4분기 수익성의 급격한 반등은 어려울 전망이
다. 향후 미국 텔루라이드·스포티지 HEV 공급 확대와 유럽 EV 판매 증가에 따른 고정
비 흡수가 수익성 회복의 핵심이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 155,000원으로 하향 
기아에 대한 투자의견 Buy를 유지하고, 실적 추정치 하향을 반영해 목표주가를 
155,000원으로 하향한다. 재료비 부담, 환율 효과 축소, 예상보다 큰 믹스 악화로 단
기 이익 눈높이 조정은 불가피하다. 다만 산업수요 둔화에도 주요 시장에서 점유율을 
확대되고 있다는 점은 긍정적이다. 현재 배당 수익률은 7% 수준으로, 상대적으로 높
은 배당 매력은 주가 하방을 지지할 전망이다. 
 Analyst 이병근 
bglee@ls-sec.co.kr 
Buy (유지)

## Document 352: European resilience, American vigour (2026-10-30)

Source PDF: all report pdf/European resilience, American vigour (2026-10-30).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 30 October 2026

EcoFlash

European resilience, American vigour

Eurozone growth continues to prove resilient, notwithstanding the volatility of the Irish figures. The Q3 data reinforce 
our forecast of 1.1% growth this year, which could even be slightly exceeded, as German and Spanish momentum shows 
no sign of fading. In France, the negative surprise (-0.1% q/q against a consensus of +0.1% q/q) stems from a new one-
off factor (the sharp fall in aerospace output caused by delays in input deliveries) and masks an encouraging rebound 
in household consumption. In the United  States, growth reached its highest level since Q2 2025 (0.8% q/q), driven by 
buoyant private domestic demand.

Eurozone: The resilience of growth is confirmed 
Eurozone GDP grew by 0.4% q/q in Q3  according to Eurostat's preliminary flash estimate, above both the consensus and our 
Nowcast (0.3%). The ever -volatile Irish figures contributed 0.2 pp to this outcome. Excluding Ireland, growth (0.2% q/q) 
nonetheless remains in line with the pace of recent quarters (0.2 -0.3% q/q on average). This illustrates the Eurozone's capacity 
to withstand the current shocks, thanks in particular to the strength of Spain (+0.07 pp contribution to the q/q change in Eurozone’s 
GDP) and the Netherlands (+0.04 pp), and to Germany's gradual recovery (+0.06 pp). France (-0.03 pp) and Italy (zero growth), 
by contrast, posted disappointing results, although in France's case these partly reflect temporary factors (aerospace output). The 
growth carry-over for 2026 now stands at 1.0%, which supports our forecast of 1.1% in annual average terms and even points to 
upside risk. The European Commission's October surveys remain upbeat, with a further improvement in industrial activity 
expectations. 
The German recovery continues . As anticipated by our  Nowcast, real GDP grew by 0.2% q/q in Q3 (after 0.3% in Q2).  
According to Destatis, consumption (private and public) increased slightly, while investment edged down. Export momentum 
slowed markedly compared with Q2. This is Germany's fourth consecutive quarter of growth. 
Italian GDP surprised on the downside, stagnating in Q3 (against a consensus of 0.1%).  According to Istat, this reflects a 
decline in value added in industry, offset by increases in services and agriculture. The positive contribution from domestic demand 
was reportedly cancelled out by a negative contribution from net trade. 
Spanish growth remained robust in Q3, in line with expectations (0.6% q/q, after 0.7% in Q2),  driven mainly by domestic 
demand (0.5 pp contribution) and, to a lesser extent, by net exports (0.1 pp). Consumption remains the main engine of growth 
(+0.6% q/q for private consumption and +0.9% for public consumption). Investment rose by 0.7%. Export gr owth (1.3%) once 
again outpaced import growth (1.1%) this quarter. All sectors except construction recorded positive changes in value added, with 
industry and services both up 0.7%. 
France: A sharp drop in aerospace output prevents a return to growth in Q3 
GDP contracted by 0.1% q/q in Q3 (after stagnating in Q2), weighed down by a sharp fall in aerospace output (-4.5% q/q 
in the production of other transport equipment, which includes aerospace). This steep decline dragged down goods production 
(down 1.2% q/q) and explains the sharp contraction in exports ( -1.7% q/q) and business investment (-0.4% q/q). Net trade thus 
subtracted 0.6 pp from GDP (imports were flat over the same period). Conversely, household consumption surprised on the 
upside (+0.4% q/q), boosted by purchases of electric vehicles (registrations up 112% y/y in Q3). Public consumption 
(+0.3% q/q) also supported growth, as did activity in market services (+0.3% q/q).  Despite the sharp drop in aerospace 
output in Q3, Airbus has confirmed its delivery targets for the year (with engine supply problems expected to be resolved), pointing 
to a strong rebound in growth in Q4 (0.3% q/q according to our forecasts). Overall, gr owth is expected to reach 0.3% in 2026 , 
before picking up to 0.8% in 2027. 
United States: Growth at a one-year high  
GDP growth picked up in Q3 to 3.4% at a seasonally adjusted annualised rate ( AR; i.e. 0.8% q/q), from 2.2% in Q2 
(0.6% q/q). This is the fastest pace since Q3 2025, confirming that activity is almost immune to the energy shock. Private domestic 
demand remains particularly strong. Private non-residential investment accelerated to 12.7% AR, a performance unmatched since 
the post-Covid reopening. AI-related components are driving this figure (18.5% AR), but other types of investment are also rising 
for a second consecutive quarter (3.8% AR) after contracting uninterruptedly throughout 2025. Household consumption, steady 
at 2.5% AR in real terms, is growing faster than it did before inflation picked up again, but at the cost of a significant drop in the 
saving rate (3.8% at the end of the quarter, down from 4.4% at the end of the previous quarter). The United States nonetheles s 
continues to "import its growth": net trade is weighing on GDP owing to a surge in imports (19.7% AR), driven by AI -related 
components – IT, semiconductors and telecommunications (103.8% AR).

### Page 2

Stéphane Colliac 
Head of Advanced Economies Team, France  
stephane.colliac@bnpparibas.com

Guillaume Derrien 
Economist, Europe 
guillaume.a.derrien@bnpparibas.com

Lucie Barette 
Economist, Germany, Italy and Spain 
lucie.barette@bnpparibas.com

Economist, United States, Japan

## Document 364: FX Daily_ Dollar holds gains into midterms

Source PDF: all report pdf/FX Daily_ Dollar holds gains into midterms.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

At the start of a very busy week for FX markets, the dollar is
largely holding onto recent gains. In focus are the US midterms,
where betting markets attach a 70% probability to a clean
sweep for the Democrats. Away from politics, we'll receive
updates on the US jobs market this week and are expecting
rate hikes in the UK, Australia and Sweden

Chris Turner, Frantisek Taborsky and Francesco Pesole
The US midterm elections are set to dominate a busy week in FX. A clean
sweep for the Democrats is expected, but the macro story can keep the dollar
supported
USD: Contemplating the midterms
The dollar is fractionally lower at the start of the week. It may be
taking its cue from oil prices, which are oﬀ 2-3% on reports that US
President Donald Trump wants to let diplomacy run its course with
Iran. That reverses Friday's sentiment, where some had positioned
for a fresh attack this weekend. Please see our latest thoughts on
the oil market here.

### Page 2

In focus this week are the US midterm elections. Betting websites
give the Democrats a 70-72% chance of taking both the House and
the Senate. One could make the case that the dollar should come
lower on such an outcome, since it would likely lead to gridlock on
the debt ceiling, a prolonged government shutdown next year and
could encourage President Trump to pursue an even more activist
foreign policy agenda. Many of those views are speculative,
however. What is not speculative is the US economy posting a

strong 3.4% quarter-on-quarter annualised growth rate in the third
quarter, inﬂation remaining above target for a sixth year in a row,
and the Federal Reserve readying to tighten again.
Feeding into the US macro debate this week will be ISM business
surveys and jobs data. Business surveys are expected to show
continued optimism, along with high price pressures. And Friday's
October jobs report is expected to show a modest rebound after a
soft September ﬁgure. Barring a wholly disastrous report on Friday,

we doubt the jobs data really moves the needle on the Fed
tightening story. Price data is more important here.
There will be continued focus on the tech sell-oﬀ, however. Apple
reports after the market close today. The 9% Nasdaq sell-oﬀ last
month has driven a softening in short-dated US rates, and any
further large sell-oﬀ in this sector is probably the biggest risk to
long dollar positioning.
Expect DXY to continue trading around 102.00, and we do not see a

strong case for a correction lower.
Chris Turner

French sovereign debt from stable to negative and promised
another ratings review by the end of February. It currently rates
France as A+. Reasons for the outlook shift are familiar by now, but
they centre on the government's failure to improve public ﬁnances

at a time of weak growth. Let's see whether this unscheduled move

GBP: 25bp BoE rate hike fully priced for
Thursday
Sterling is trading reasonably well. Helping it has been the view that

high energy prices leave the Bank of England no alternative but to
hike rates on Thursday. A 25bp rate hike to 4.00% looks like a done
deal. Upside economic surprises, UK ﬁscal policy seemingly
heading in the right direction and warmer ties with the EU all seem
to be helping sterling too. Notably, speculators are still running
reasonably large short positions in sterling, which suggests this
period of sterling outperformance can continue.
EUR/GBP has come a long way very quickly – a near 4% drop in a

little over a month. Some consolidation is probably due. But with
high energy prices likely to keep expectations of higher UK policy
rates in place until early next year and French budgetary problems
unresolved, it is too early to expect a signiﬁcant low in EUR/GBP.

released. Since the onset of the US-Iran conﬂict, these ﬁgures have
either remained stagnant or trended upward, indicating that the
economies are currently showing strong resilience to the energy
shock.

Charts of the week: No capitulation

A hotter-than-expected US inflation report triggered a pullback in risk assets, but there are few signs of capitulation so far 
US core and supercore inflation; US inflation breadth*    Our ‘Fear and Greed’ indicator

Source: Bloomberg, Standard Chartered; *share of US consumer inflation components rising more than 3%

Editorial 
Staying defensive and diversified  
Strategy summary:  Two weeks ago, we turned defensive 
towards equities, locking in semiconductor equity gains amid 
elevated bond volatility, narrowing market breadth and political 
uncertainty before the US mid -term elections. The near -term 
outlook reflected a less favourable risk-reward balance. Since 
we turned defensive , the Nasdaq 100 index has recorded its 
biggest weekly decline in three months as September’s hotter-
than-expected US inflation report briefly lifted bond yields. 
We have extended our defensive stance this week, 
downgrading US Communication Services equities to Neutral 
and upgrading US Healthcare to Overweight. We also closed 
our opportunistic bullish Communication Services idea and 
initiated a bullish idea on Healthcare, where earnings visibility 
and defensive characteristics appear more attractive. In fixed 
income, we see opportunities in US 10-year government bonds. 
We await clearer AI profit margins and spending guidance, 
broader earnings growth or lower bond and equity market 
volatility before turning positive on equities. Until then, 
diversification and disciplined entry levels remain important. 
Hot US inflation triggers equity pullback; not hot enough 
for an October Fed hike:  Risk assets pulled back after 
September’s hotter-than-expected inflation data (headline 3.7% 
y/y; core 2.6% y/y). Higher gasoline and diesel prices were the 
main drivers, but passthrough appeared in airline fares, used 
cars and transportation services, showing pressures were not 
confined to energy. Almost half of inflation components are still 
rising above 3%, a measure recently cited by Fed Chair Warsh. 
Producer inflation also beat estimates. The reports revive the 
possibility of an October Fed rate hike, but we expect a divided 
Fed to weigh it against September’s soft jobs report and 
narrowly hold this month. A December hike appears more likely 
if inflation remains firm . Although Fed Chair Warsh did not 
comment on policy at an IMF speech this week, New York Fed 
chief Williams said he saw no need to hike in 2027. Our base 
case remains two further hikes by June 2027. 
Limited equity pullback so far.  Equities fell sharply after the 
US inflation report, but the pullback is limited. The Nasdaq 100 
is 4.5% below its record high, while the S&P 500 remains close 
to all -time highs despite the latest decline. The S&P 500 
volatility index (VIX) has risen from below 15 to 1 8.2, still well 
under the 25 –30 range typically associated with capitulation 
and forced selling. Meanwhile, US government bond volatility 
(MOVE) remains elevated after surging since September.  Our 
stock-bond quant model turned more negativ e (-2) this week, 
signalling elevated equity volatility in the near term.  
When to turn more constructive on stocks?  We would turn 
more constructive when capitulation signals align: the Nasdaq 
100 approaches a 10% drawdown, the VIX rises to 25 –30 and 
our ‘Fear and Greed’ index  enters “extreme fear”. Greater 
confidence would also require easing concerns about AI 
spending and technology firms’ exposure to neocloud revenue, 
lower bond volatility and our stock-bond model turning positive 
again. Until these conditions emerge, volatility may remain 
elevated, favouring a defensive equity stance. Political risk 
should ease after the US mid -term elections. We continue to 
seek selective opportunities following excessive corrections. 
US financials remain a preferred sector after Q3 earnings:  
US large banks’ earnings were mixed, but underlying trends 
remain encouraging. Healthy loan and deposit growth supports 
net interest income, while resilient consumer spending, wealth 
management activity and a solid M&A and refinancing pipeline 
strengthen the outlook. Credit risks appear manageable . We 
remain constructive and favour stronger, diversified franchises. 
US government bonds offer value as yields spike:  The 
reversal in US government bond yields after their initial inflation-
driven spike suggests limited further upside. The 10 -year yield 
briefly reached 5.32% before slumping as falling equities 
prompted short-covering. We expect a near -term 5.00–5.25% 
range and view moves above 5.25% as attractive entry points 
for our opportunistic 10-year government bond idea. Structural 
pressures, including term premium and increasing supply to 
fund fiscal deficits and AI investments, prevent us from turning 
outright bullish on longer maturities. We, therefore, continue to 
prefer 3–7-year maturities in broader bond allocations.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya and Sundeep Gantori

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 16 October 2026

Top client questions 
  How does the September 2026 US Consumer Price Index 
(CPI) report alter your view on the USD and bond yields?   
Our view: The hotter-than-expected CPI print raises the odds of 
an October Fed hike, though we still expect a hold followed by 
a rate hike in December. In the near term, we maintain our view 
that the US 10 -year government bond yield will range between 
5.00% and 5.25%, while  the USD will remain supported as 
markets reassess the likelihood of further Fed hikes. Additional 
gains would depend partly on markets pricing a faster or more 
prolonged tightening cycle, while clearer signs of an 
approaching policy rate peak could see the USD ease back. 
Rationale: September US consumer inflation  came in above 
consensus across the board – headline CPI rose 3.7% y/y (vs. 3.6% 
estimate), and core CPI rose 2.6% y/y (vs. 2.5% estimate) – marking 
the fastest headline acceleration in five months. This shifts the 
balance of risks modestly hawkish . Unsurprisingly, the market -
implied probability of an October Fed hike rose over 50% overnight 
but closed the day at 32.7% – 9ppt higher than Tuesday’s close. 
We believe a split Fed will weigh this inflation report against the soft 
September jobs report and narrowly vote to keep rates on hold this 
month. Our base case remains for two more Fed hikes  by June 
2027. A December rate hike  now looks more like a done deal, 
especially as persistently higher oil prices keep headline and core 
inflation elevated for at least the next few months. Any re-escalation 
of the Middle East conflict after the  US mid-term elections would 
exert pressure on the Fed, although we believe Republicans would  
show greater restraint in US foreign action if they suffer steep Senate 
losses, which looks increasingly likely. 
The intra-day price action in US government bonds was instructive. 
The US 10-year yield briefly spiked to 5.32% on the print before 
closing materially lower, as pre -existing bearish positioning was 
unwound and Fed speakers (Barkin, Goolsbee) stopped well short 
of signalling an imminent move. This dynamic reinforces our view 
that the technical selling pressure driving yields towards the upper 
end of our range is fading. Structural drivers – term premium, fiscal 
supply concerns and ‘higher for longer’ Fed repricing – remain intact 
and keep us from turning outright bullish on duration. However, our 
opportunistic bullish idea in US 10-year government bonds initiated 
at September-end remains, as we continue to view spikes in US 10-
year yields over 5.25% as a good risk-reward entry. From a longer-
term portfolio standpoint, we continue to favour the 3-7-year tenor. 
On FX, elevated US government bond yields should sustain the 
USD’s interest rate appeal. However, expected hikes alone will not 
drive further appreciation. Stronger data or firmer Fed guidance 
would provide fresh support, while evidence that tightening is 
nearing completion would temper the USD’s strength. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
— Ray Heung, Senior Investment Strategist

rangebound between 102 and 103, supported by 
elevated US government bond yields

### Page 5

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 16 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What are your key takeaways from US bank earnings? 
Our view: Q3 2026 earnings results from major US banks have 
been mixed, but underlying trends remain encouraging. Healthy 
loan and deposit growth, resilient consumer spending and 
wealth management activity continue to support the sector. 
Credit risks remain manageable. We remain constructive on the 
US financial sector, favouring stronger, diversified franchises. 
Rationale: Healthy loan and deposit growth continue to support net 
interest income (NII), hinting that the US economy remains resilient. 
Capital markets revenue grew year -on-year (y/y) , but moderated 
sequentially after a very strong Q2 2026, although performance 
varied across banks. Management commentary points to a healthy 
deal pipeline , supported by M&A activity and debt refinancing 
demand, although execution will depend on market conditions. 
Provisioning and net charge -off trends were mixed across banks, 
with no clear signs of broad deterioration in credit quality. Firmer 
US inflation raises the risk of further monetary tightening. While 
higher rates can support asset yields, they can also increase 
funding costs, pressure borrowers and weigh on securities ’ 
valuations. We will continue to monitor lending demand, credit 
costs, NII trends, capital markets momentum and capital returns.  
—  Robin Xie, Senior Investment Strategist 
  Can Big Tech’s earnings guidance sustain confidence in 
the AI investment cycle? 
Our view: We expect Big Tech’s AI spending to remain elevated, 
supporting our constructive AI capex outlook. However, we 
await guidance on the pace of capex growth and the 
sustainability of profit margins. 
Rationale: Semiconductor leaders’ latest company guidance points 
to supply constraints extending through 2028, supporting continued 
capacity expansion for compute . However, while AI capex is still 
likely to rise, y/y growth is expected to moderate  from c.100% in 
Q3 2026 to c.20% in Q3 2027. This normalisation in growth could 
raise concerns about the AI investment cycle’s durability.  
The Nasdaq-100 Index has rallied alongside accelerating AI capex. 
Slower capex growth is likely to moderate semiconductor revenue 
growth, while hyperscalers continue to incur depreciation costs on 
past investments. The slower growth is already partially reflected in 
the Nasdaq -100’s valuation de -rating this year. While we remain 
constructive on the AI investment theme, we prefer to stay 
prudent in the near term and await Big Tech’s guidance for greater 
visibility into spending plans in H2 2027 and beyond. 
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist

### Page 6

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 16 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Would France face a credit rating downgrade by rating 
agencies this month? What might be the market implications? 
Our view: A rating downgrade is unlikely unless France’s 2027 
budget passes the National Assembly very soon with material 
changes to the proposal. We see French government bond 
yields remaining elevated relative to German Bund yields. 
Rationale: The French National Assembly began debating France’s 
2027 draft budget on 13 October 2026, with the first vote scheduled 
for 20 October. Near-term budget approval appears unlikely, as 
opposition parties have tabled motions to reject the proposal. The 
French Parliament has up to 70 days from the budget’s submission 
to complete the legislative process, although  its final adoption and 
the resolution of political challenges could extend into early 2027.  
Last October, two of the three major rating agencies downgraded 
France by one notch to the high-single-A category  with stable 
outlooks. The remaining agenc y rated France in the low-AA 
category with a negative outlook. As negative outlooks are typically 
resolved within 12 -18 months, agenc ies have time to await fiscal 
clarity before acting. We believe downgrade risks are largely priced 
in after the recent rise in yields, but volatility is likely to persist amid 
fiscal uncertainty and the approaching presidential election. Banks 
and domestically-focused issuers would face the greatest near-term 
pressure. However, we would view wider spreads as an opportunity 
to add exposure, particularly to French banks’ subordinated 
debt, given their stronger balance sheets and the fact that France 
contributes less than 50% of major French banks’ overall revenue. 
—  Ray Heung, Senior Investment Strategist 
  Are you still positive on Japanese banks? 
Our view: We are positive on Japanese banks despite the recent 
market correction, as the structural drivers of improving 
profitability and shareholder returns remain intact . While 
political risk and shifting BoJ rate hike odds could sustain near-
term volatility, we see the recent pullback as an opportunity to 
build selective exposure to Japanese bank stocks. 
Rationale: Japan’s gradual monetary policy normalisation  
should support banks’ NII as floating-rate loans reprice and maturing 
assets are reinvested at higher yields. Healthy corporate lending 
demand, improving capital efficiency and continued share buybacks 
provide further support . While uncertainty over the pace of BoJ 
tightening could weigh on sentiment, we see gradual interest rate 
normalisation as supportive of medium-term bank profitability.  
Key risks  include rising deposit costs, mark -to-market losses on 
bond portfolios and a disorderly increase in Japanese government 
bond yields. We continue to favour well-capitalised banks  with 
strong domestic deposit franchises and improving returns on equity. 
—  Robin Xie, Senior Investment Strategist

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Could NZD/USD rebound from its one-year trough? If so, 
how far could it recover?  
Our view: New Zealand’s improving domestic conditions, 
persistent inflation and technical support provide a basis for 
NZD/USD’s initial recovery towards the 0.57 level, although 
elevated US bond yields could slow its progress. 
Rationale: In Q3, New Zealand’s  seasonally-adjusted business 
confidence reading rose to a net 40% of firms expecting the economy 
to improve, up from net 14% in Q2 . Hiring intentions improved, and 
filled jobs rose 0.2% m/m in August. These developments suggest 
domestic conditions remain supportive for the NZD.  
New Zealand’s above-target inflation keeps further RBNZ tightening 
in focus. The 22 October consumer inflation data release and the 
28 October RBNZ policy meeting are key catalysts: persistent price 
pressures a nd improving demand are expected to prompt firmer 
policy guidance and strengthen the NZD’s interest-rate appeal. 
NZD/USD’s position near the lower boundary of its downtrend 
channel suggests scope for a technical rebound. We see 0.57 as 
the first recovery hurdle; a sustained break  above this level  would 
strengthen the case for further gains. Firmer US  consumer inflation 
remains a headwind, making a gradual recovery more plausible 
than a sharp reversal.  
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist 
  Can the proposed Russia -Ukraine ‘energy ceasefire ’ 
contain oil prices as Middle East risks rise? 
Our view: We expect escalating Middle East tensions to keep oil 
prices elevated . We  maintain our  three- and 12 -month West 
Texas Intermediate (WTI) oil price forecasts at USD 100/bbl and 
USD 80/bbl, respectively, and continue to favour US Treasury 
Inflation-Protected Securities (TIPS) as a portfolio hedge 
against persistent inflation risks. 
Rationale: The proposed Russia-Ukraine ‘energy ceasefire’ still 
looks some way off. In our view, even if an agreement is reached, it 
would do little to ease the supply risks building in the Middle East. 
Gulf oil exports have recovered to above 80% of pre-conflict levels 
in recent weeks, helping moderate the pace of price increases. 
However, the recovery remains vulnerable to disruptions, particularly 
following the latest Houthi attacks on Saudi energy infrastructure . 
Diesel and jet fuel remain under the most pressure , with Gulf 
exports of these fuels in September  at only around 60% of pre -
conflict levels. A sustained disruption to major Saudi production or 
export infrastructure remains the principal upside risk to oil prices. 
—  Anthony Naab, Investment Strategist
